```text
4. Replace AlarmScreen.tsx (full file — haptics on arm/disarm)
5. Test in Expo Go
6. Commit
7. Confirm app.json edits from earlier
8. Build + install APK
```


## Step 1: Add the alarm tone file — do this first

I cannot generate an actual audio file in text. On your phone or laptop browser, search "free alarm sound mp3 download," grab any short clip (a few seconds is fine, it will loop), and get it onto your computer as an `.mp3`.

In Codespaces:

1. In the Explorer panel, right-click the `assets` folder.
2. If Codespaces gives you an upload option, use it. If not, drag the file from your computer directly onto the `assets` folder in the Explorer.
3. Rename it exactly to:
```text
assets/alarm-tone.mp3
```

**This must exist before you paste the code below**, or Metro will throw "Unable to resolve module" the moment you save `RingingScreen.tsx`.

## Step 2: Install packages

```bash
npx expo install expo-haptics expo-audio
```

Both are fully supported in Expo Go — no rebuild needed yet for this stage.

## Step 3: Replace `src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Pressable, Modal } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import { Accelerometer } from 'expo-sensors';
import { useAudioPlayer } from 'expo-audio';
import * as Haptics from 'expo-haptics';
import { colors } from '../theme/colors';
import { useSettings } from '../context/SettingsContext';
import { useWakeFlow } from '../context/WakeFlowContext';

const SNOOZE_PIN = '8492017356';

export default function RingingScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const {
    wakeFlow,
    incrementMovement,
    dismissWake,
    startSnooze,
    decrementSnooze,
    returnToRingingFromSnooze,
  } = useWakeFlow();

  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(new Date());
  const [sensorAvailable, setSensorAvailable] = useState(true);

  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [wrongBarcodeMessage, setWrongBarcodeMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const scanLockRef = useRef(false);

  const [isKeypadOpen, setIsKeypadOpen] = useState(false);
  const [enteredPin, setEnteredPin] = useState('');
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState(false);

  const lastAccelRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; z: number } | null>(null);
  const lastMovementTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const progressRef = useRef(wakeFlow.movementProgress);

  const player = useAudioPlayer(require('../../assets/alarm-tone.mp3'));

  useEffect(() => {
    progressRef.current = wakeFlow.movementProgress;
  }, [wakeFlow.movementProgress]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => setCurrentTime(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing') {
      player.loop = true;
      player.seekTo(0);
      player.play();
    } else {
      player.pause();
    }

    return () => {
      player.pause();
    };
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'ringing') return;

    Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Warning);

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Warning);
    }, 2800);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  const isMovementComplete = !settings.movementEnabled || wakeFlow.movementProgress >= settings.movementTarget;

  useEffect(() => {
    let subscription: { remove: () => void } | null = null;
    let isActive = true;

    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'ringing' || !settings.movementEnabled) {
      return;
    }

    const setup = async () => {
      const available = await Accelerometer.isAvailableAsync();
      if (!isActive) return;
      setSensorAvailable(available);
      if (!available) return;

      Accelerometer.setUpdateInterval(120);
      subscription = Accelerometer.addListener((data: { x: number; y: number; z: number }) => {
        const { x, y, z } = data;
        const last = lastAccelRef.current;

        if (last) {
          const delta = Math.abs(x - last.x) + Math.abs(y - last.y) + Math.abs(z - last.z);
          const now = Date.now();

          if (delta > 0.7 && now - lastMovementTimeRef.current > 200 && progressRef.current < settings.movementTarget) {
            lastMovementTimeRef.current = now;
            incrementMovement(settings.movementTarget);
          }
        }

        lastAccelRef.current = { x, y, z };
      });
    };

    setup();

    return () => {
      isActive = false;
      if (subscription) {
        subscription.remove();
      }
    };
  }, [wakeFlow.status, settings.movementEnabled, settings.movementTarget]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'snoozed') return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      decrementSnooze();
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'snoozed' && wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining === 0) {
      returnToRingingFromSnooze();
    }
  }, [wakeFlow.status, wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining]);

  const handleOpenDisarmScanner = async () => {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing' && !isMovementComplete) return;

    scanLockRef.current = false;
    setWrongBarcodeMessage(null);

    if (!permission?.granted) {
      const result = await requestPermission();
      if (!result.granted) {
        setIsScannerOpen(true);
        return;
      }
    }

    setIsScannerOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDisarmBarcodeScanned = (result: { data: string }) => {
    if (scanLockRef.current) return;

    const scanned = result.data.trim();
    if (!scanned) return;

    if (scanned === settings.wakeBarcode) {
      scanLockRef.current = true;
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Success);
      setIsScannerOpen(false);
      dismissWake();
      return;
    }

    scanLockRef.current = true;
    Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Error);
    setWrongBarcodeMessage(`Wrong item. Scan your ${settings.wakeItemName || 'registered wake-up item'}.`);
    setTimeout(() => {
      scanLockRef.current = false;
    }, 1500);
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (digit: string) => {
    if (enteredPin.length < 10) {
      setEnteredPin((prev) => prev + digit);
      setPinError(false);
    }
  };

  const handleClearPin = () => {
    setEnteredPin('');
    setPinError(false);
  };

  const handleConfirmPin = () => {
    if (enteredPin === SNOOZE_PIN) {
      Haptics.impactAsync(Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Medium);
      setEnteredPin('');
      setPinError(false);
      setIsKeypadOpen(false);
      startSnooze();
    } else {
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Error);
      setPinError(true);
      setEnteredPin('');
    }
  };

  const timeFormatted = currentTime.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false });
  const minutes = Math.floor(wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining / 60);
  const seconds = wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining % 60;
  const snoozeTimeFormatted = `${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top, paddingBottom: insets.bottom }]}>
      {wakeFlow.status === 'ringing' ? (
        <>
          <View style={styles.topSection}>
            <View style={styles.statusPill}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>Alarm Active</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.clockText}>{timeFormatted}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subText}>
              Scan <Text style={styles.subTextBold}>{settings.wakeItemName || 'your registered item'}</Text> to
              disarm
            </Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.middleSection}>
            {settings.movementEnabled && (
              <View style={styles.card}>
                <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Physical Movement</Text>

                {sensorAvailable ? (
                  <>
                    <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
                      {wakeFlow.movementProgress} / {settings.movementTarget}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.progressFill,
                          { width: `${Math.min(100, (wakeFlow.movementProgress / settings.movementTarget) * 100)}%` },
                        ]}
                      />
                    </View>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Motion sensor unavailable. Use manual fallback.</Text>
                    <Pressable
                      style={styles.fallbackButton}
                      onPress={() => incrementMovement(settings.movementTarget)}
                      accessibilityLabel="Simulate movement, sensor unavailable"
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.fallbackButtonText}>
                        Tap to simulate movement ({wakeFlow.movementProgress}/{settings.movementTarget})
                      </Text>
                    </Pressable>
                  </>
                )}
              </View>
            )}

            {wrongBarcodeMessage && (
              <View style={styles.errorBanner}>
                <Text style={styles.errorBannerText}>{wrongBarcodeMessage}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.scanButton, !isMovementComplete && styles.scanButtonDisabled]}
              onPress={handleOpenDisarmScanner}
              disabled={!isMovementComplete}
              accessibilityLabel="Scan to Disarm"
            >
              <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>
                {isMovementComplete ? 'Scan to Disarm' : 'Complete Movement First'}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.snoozeButton}
              onPress={() => setIsKeypadOpen(true)}
              accessibilityLabel="Snooze with PIN"
            >
              <Text style={styles.snoozeButtonText}>Snooze with PIN</Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.exitPreviewButton}
              onPress={dismissWake}
              accessibilityLabel="Exit preview, testing only"
            >
              <Text style={styles.exitPreviewButtonText}>Exit Preview (Testing Only)</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <View style={styles.topSection}>
            <View style={[styles.statusPill, styles.statusPillSnooze]}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>Snoozed</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.snoozeCountdownText}>{snoozeTimeFormatted}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subText}>Alarm will ring again in</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.middleSection}>
            {wrongBarcodeMessage && (
              <View style={styles.errorBanner}>
                <Text style={styles.errorBannerText}>{wrongBarcodeMessage}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.scanButton}
              onPress={handleOpenDisarmScanner}
              accessibilityLabel="Scan to Disarm Now"
            >
              <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>Scan to Disarm Now</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </>
      )}

      <Modal
        visible={isScannerOpen}
        animationType="slide"
        transparent={false}
        onRequestClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { paddingTop: insets.top, paddingBottom: insets.bottom }]}>
          {!permission?.granted ? (
            <View style={styles.permissionContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.permissionTitle}>Camera Permission Required</Text>
              <Text style={styles.permissionText}>Camera access is needed to scan your wake-up item.</Text>

              <View style={styles.modalFooter}>
                <Pressable
                  style={styles.cancelButton}
                  onPress={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.cameraWrapper}>
              <CameraView
                style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
                facing="back"
                barcodeScannerSettings={{
                  barcodeTypes: ['ean13', 'ean8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e', 'code128', 'code39', 'qr'],
                }}
                onBarcodeScanned={handleDisarmBarcodeScanned}
              />

              <View style={styles.cameraOverlay} pointerEvents="box-none">
                <View style={styles.cameraHeaderBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraHeaderText}>Scan {settings.wakeItemName}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraSubHeaderText}>Position the barcode in the center</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.cameraFooter}>
                  <Pressable
                    style={styles.cancelButton}
                    onPress={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
                    accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        visible={isKeypadOpen}
        animationType="fade"
        transparent={true}
        onRequestClose={() => setIsKeypadOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.keypadOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.keypadContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.keypadTitle}>Snooze PIN</Text>

            <View style={[styles.pinDisplay, pinError && styles.pinDisplayError]}>
              <Text style={styles.pinDisplayText}>{enteredPin.padEnd(10, '·')}</Text>
            </View>

            {pinError && <Text style={styles.pinErrorText}>Incorrect PIN. Please try again.</Text>}

            <View style={styles.keypadGrid}>
              {['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9'].map((digit) => (
                <Pressable
                  key={digit}
                  style={styles.keypadKey}
                  onPress={() => handleKeyPress(digit)}
                  accessibilityLabel={`Digit ${digit}`}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.keypadKeyText}>{digit}</Text>
                </Pressable>
              ))}

              <Pressable style={styles.keypadKeyClear} onPress={handleClearPin} accessibilityLabel="Clear PIN">
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyClearText}>CLEAR</Text>
              </Pressable>

              <Pressable style={styles.keypadKey} onPress={() => handleKeyPress('0')} accessibilityLabel="Digit 0">
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyText}>0</Text>
              </Pressable>

              <Pressable
                style={[styles.keypadKeyConfirm, enteredPin.length !== 10 && styles.keypadKeyConfirmDisabled]}
                onPress={handleConfirmPin}
                disabled={enteredPin.length !== 10}
                accessibilityLabel="Confirm PIN"
              >
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyConfirmText}>OK</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </View>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.keypadCancel}
              onPress={() => {
                setIsKeypadOpen(false);
                setEnteredPin('');
                setPinError(false);
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Cancel snooze"
            >
              <Text style={styles.keypadCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: 20 },
  topSection: { alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 24 },
  statusPill: { backgroundColor: colors.card, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 20, marginBottom: 16 },
  statusPillSnooze: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.blueAccent },
  statusPillText: { color: colors.amberAccent, fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 13 },
  clockText: { fontSize: 72, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, letterSpacing: -2 },
  snoozeCountdownText: { fontSize: 72, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.blueAccent, letterSpacing: -2 },
  subText: { fontSize: 15, color: colors.textSecondary, marginTop: 8, textAlign: 'center' },
  subTextBold: { color: colors.amberAccent, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  middleSection: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', gap: 16 },
  card: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 24, padding: 20 },
  cardTitle: { color: colors.text, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 8 },
  cardSubtitle: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 12 },
  progressTrack: { height: 10, backgroundColor: colors.background, borderRadius: 5, overflow: 'hidden' },
  progressFill: { height: 10, backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent },
  fallbackButton: { backgroundColor: colors.background, borderRadius: 16, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center' },
  fallbackButtonText: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  errorBanner: { backgroundColor: '#3a0f14', borderColor: colors.roseAccent, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 16, padding: 14 },
  errorBannerText: { color: colors.roseAccent, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'center' },
  bottomSection: { paddingBottom: 12, gap: 10 },
  scanButton: { backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent, borderRadius: 20, paddingVertical: 18, alignItems: 'center' },
  scanButtonDisabled: { backgroundColor: '#333333', opacity: 0.7 },
  scanButtonText: { color: '#000000', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  snoozeButton: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 16, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center' },
  snoozeButtonText: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  exitPreviewButton: { paddingVertical: 10, alignItems: 'center' },
  exitPreviewButtonText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12 },

  modalContainer: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  permissionContainer: { flex: 1, padding: 24, justifyContent: 'center' },
  permissionTitle: { fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginBottom: 12, textAlign: 'center' },
  permissionText: { fontSize: 16, color: colors.textSecondary, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 32, lineHeight: 24 },
  modalFooter: { marginTop: 'auto' },
  cancelButton: { paddingVertical: 16, alignItems: 'center' },
  cancelButtonText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold' },

  cameraWrapper: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },
  cameraOverlay: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'space-between', backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' },
  cameraHeaderBox: { padding: 24, paddingTop: 60, alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)' },
  cameraHeaderText: { color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 8 },
  cameraSubHeaderText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14 },
  cameraFooter: { padding: 32, paddingBottom: 48, alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)' },

  keypadOverlay: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.75)', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 24 },
  keypadContainer: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 24, padding: 24 },
  keypadTitle: { color: colors.amberAccent, fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 14, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 12, textTransform: 'uppercase' },

  pinDisplayError: { borderColor: colors.roseAccent, borderWidth: 1 },
  pinDisplayText: { color: colors.amberAccent, textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold', letterSpacing: 4 },
  pinErrorText: { color: colors.roseAccent, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  keypadGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 },
  keypadKey: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: '#222', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  keypadKeyText: { color: colors.text, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  keypadKeyClear: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: '#1a1a1a', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },

  keypadKeyConfirm: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
```

## Step 4: Replace `src/screens/AlarmScreen.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Pressable, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import * as Haptics from 'expo-haptics';
import { colors } from '../theme/colors';
import { useSettings } from '../context/SettingsContext';
import { useWakeFlow } from '../context/WakeFlowContext';

const DAYS = ['S', 'M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S'];
const FULL_DAY_NAMES = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'];

export default function AlarmScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { settings, updateSettings } = useSettings();
  const { startPreview } = useWakeFlow();
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(new Date());

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentTime(new Date());
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const formatLiveTime = (date: Date) => {
    const hh = String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const mm = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${hh}:${mm}`;
  };

  const toggleDay = (dayIndex: number) => {
    if (settings.isArmed) return;
    const isSelected = settings.repeatDays.includes(dayIndex);
    const newDays = isSelected
      ? settings.repeatDays.filter((d) => d !== dayIndex)
      : [...settings.repeatDays, dayIndex].sort();
    updateSettings({ repeatDays: newDays });
  };

  const adjustHour = (delta: number) => {
    if (settings.isArmed) return;
    let newHour = settings.alarmHour + delta;
    if (newHour > 23) newHour = 0;
    if (newHour < 0) newHour = 23;
    updateSettings({ alarmHour: newHour });
  };

  const adjustMinute = (delta: number) => {
    if (settings.isArmed) return;

    let newMinute = settings.alarmMinute + delta;
    let newHour = settings.alarmHour;

    if (newMinute > 59) {
      newMinute = 0;
      newHour += 1;
      if (newHour > 23) newHour = 0;
    } else if (newMinute < 0) {
      newMinute = 59;
      newHour -= 1;
      if (newHour < 0) newHour = 23;
    }

    updateSettings({ alarmHour: newHour, alarmMinute: newMinute });
  };

  const toggleArmed = () => {
    if (settings.repeatDays.length === 0 && !settings.isArmed) return;

    const nextArmed = !settings.isArmed;
    updateSettings({ isArmed: nextArmed });

    Haptics.impactAsync(
      nextArmed ? Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Medium : Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Light
    );
  };

  const noDaysSelected = settings.repeatDays.length === 0;
  const isArmButtonDisabled = noDaysSelected && !settings.isArmed;

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      <View style={styles.clockContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.clockText}>{formatLiveTime(currentTime)}</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Wake-up Time</Text>
        <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
          {settings.isArmed ? 'Alarm is armed and locked' : 'Adjust your wake-up time'}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.timeControlsContainer}>
          <View style={styles.timeSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.adjustButton}
              onPress={() => adjustHour(-1)}
              accessibilityLabel="Decrease hour"
              disabled={settings.isArmed}
            >
              <Text style={styles.adjustButtonText}>-</Text>
            </Pressable>
            <Text style={styles.alarmTimeText}>{String(settings.alarmHour).padStart(2, '0')}</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.adjustButton}
              onPress={() => adjustHour(1)}
              accessibilityLabel="Increase hour"
              disabled={settings.isArmed}
            >
              <Text style={styles.adjustButtonText}>+</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.timeColon}>:</Text>

          <View style={styles.timeSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.adjustButton}
              onPress={() => adjustMinute(-1)}
              accessibilityLabel="Decrease minute"
              disabled={settings.isArmed}
            >
              <Text style={styles.adjustButtonText}>-</Text>
            </Pressable>
            <Text style={styles.alarmTimeText}>{String(settings.alarmMinute).padStart(2, '0')}</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.adjustButton}
              onPress={() => adjustMinute(1)}
              accessibilityLabel="Increase minute"
              disabled={settings.isArmed}
            >
              <Text style={styles.adjustButtonText}>+</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.chipContainer}>
          {DAYS.map((day, index) => {
            const isSelected = settings.repeatDays.includes(index);
            return (
              <Pressable
                key={index}
                style={[styles.chip, isSelected && styles.chipActive, settings.isArmed && styles.chipDisabled]}
                onPress={() => toggleDay(index)}
                accessibilityLabel={`Toggle ${FULL_DAY_NAMES[index]}`}
                accessibilityState={{ selected: isSelected }}
                disabled={settings.isArmed}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.chipText, isSelected && styles.chipTextActive]}>{day}</Text>
              </Pressable>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {noDaysSelected && <Text style={styles.warningText}>No repeat days selected</Text>}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.spacer} />

      <Pressable
        style={[
          styles.primaryButton,
          isArmButtonDisabled ? styles.primaryButtonDisabled : null,
          settings.isArmed ? styles.primaryButtonCancel : null,
        ]}
        onPress={toggleArmed}
        disabled={isArmButtonDisabled}
        accessibilityLabel={settings.isArmed ? 'Cancel Alarm' : 'Arm Alarm'}
        accessibilityHint={
          isArmButtonDisabled ? 'Select at least one repeat day before arming the alarm.' : undefined
        }
      >
        <Text style={styles.primaryButtonText}>{settings.isArmed ? 'Cancel Alarm' : 'Arm Alarm'}</Text>
      </Pressable>

      {settings.isArmed && (
        <>
          <Pressable
            style={styles.previewButton}
            onPress={startPreview}
            accessibilityLabel="Preview Wake-up Flow"
          >
            <Text style={styles.previewButtonText}>Preview Wake-up Flow</Text>
          </Pressable>

          <Text style={styles.previewHint}>
            This preview simulates the alarm while the app is open. It does not yet run in the background.
          </Text>
        </>
      )}
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flexGrow: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: 16 },
  clockContainer: { alignItems: 'center', marginVertical: 40, justifyContent: 'center' },
  clockText: { fontSize: 80, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, letterSpacing: -2 },
  card: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 24, padding: 24, marginBottom: 24 },
  cardTitle: { fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginBottom: 4 },
  cardSubtitle: { fontSize: 14, color: colors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 24 },
  timeControlsContainer: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 24 },
  timeSection: { alignItems: 'center' },
  alarmTimeText: { fontSize: 48, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginVertical: 8 },
  adjustButton: { backgroundColor: colors.background, width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  adjustButtonText: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  timeColon: { fontSize: 48, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginHorizontal: 16 },
  chipContainer: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' },
  chip: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 20, backgroundColor: colors.background, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  chipActive: { backgroundColor: colors.blueAccent },
  chipDisabled: { opacity: 0.5 },
  chipText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 16 },
  chipTextActive: { color: colors.text },
  warningText: { color: colors.amberAccent, marginTop: 16, textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 'bold' },
  spacer: { flex: 1 },
  primaryButton: { backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent, borderRadius: 24, paddingVertical: 18, alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 },
  primaryButtonCancel: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.amberAccent },
  primaryButtonDisabled: { backgroundColor: '#333333', opacity: 0.7 },
  primaryButtonText: { color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  previewButton: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.blueAccent, borderRadius: 20, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 8 },
  previewButtonText: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  previewHint: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 24, lineHeight: 18 },
});
```


## Step 5: Test

```bash
npx tsc --noEmit
npx expo start --tunnel --clear
```

Arm → Preview → confirm the tone loops and the phone vibrates in pulses → snooze with `8492017356` → confirm sound/vibration stop → wait for countdown → confirm it rings again → scan the correct barcode → confirm success vibration and dismissal.

## Step 6: Commit

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Add alarm sound and haptic feedback"
git push origin main
```


## Step 7: Confirm app.json (from before you build)

Make sure `app.json` has both of these inside `"expo": {}`:

```json
"android": {
  "package": "com.rainbowblurry.wakeupnative"
},
"plugins": [
  [
    "expo-camera",
    { "cameraPermission": "WakeUp needs camera access to scan your registered wake-up item barcode." }
  ]
]
```


## Step 8: Build the APK

```bash
npm install -g eas-cli
eas login
eas build:configure
```

Ensure `eas.json`'s `preview` profile has `"android": { "buildType": "apk" }`, then:

```bash
eas build --platform android --profile preview
```

Install the resulting APK on your Samsung and test it standalone — alarm, sound, haptics, camera, disarm — all without Expo Go. Report back and we go straight into 4F.

---

# @GitHub Android Bundling failed 14904ms index.ts (1051 modules)

Unable to resolve "../../assets/alarm-tone.mp3" from "src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx"
40 | const progressRef = useRef(wakeFlow.movementProgress);
41 |

> 42 | const player = useAudioPlayer(require('../../assets/alarm-tone.mp3'));
> | ^
> 43 |
> 44 | useEffect(() => {
> 45 | progressRef.current = wakeFlow.movementProgress;

Import stack:

src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx
| import "../../assets/alarm-tone.mp3"

App.tsx
| import "./src/screens/RingingScreen"

index.ts
| import "./App"

| import "./index.ts"

This confirms exactly what I warned about: `assets/alarm-tone.mp3` doesn't exist in the repository yet. Metro cannot bundle a `require()` for a file that isn't there. This isn't a code bug — the code is correct, the asset is missing.

## Fix it now

### Step 1: Get a short audio file

On your phone or laptop browser, search for something like "free alarm sound mp3 no copyright" and download any short clip (2–10 seconds is plenty since it loops). Save it to your device.

### Step 2: Add it to the repo — two options

**Option A — GitHub website (most reliable):**

1. Go to `https://github.com/rainbowblurry-book/alarmG-native`
2. Open the `assets` folder
3. Click **Add file → Upload files**
4. Drag your downloaded audio file in
5. Rename it in the upload screen to exactly:

```text
alarm-tone.mp3
```

6. Commit directly to `main`

**Option B — Drag into Codespaces Explorer:**

1. Open the Codespaces browser tab
2. In the Explorer panel, expand `assets`
3. Drag the mp3 file from your computer's file manager directly onto the `assets` folder row

### Step 3: Sync Codespaces (only if you used Option A)

If you uploaded via the GitHub website, your Codespace doesn't have it yet. Run:

```bash
git pull origin main
```

If you used Option B (dragged directly into Codespaces), skip this — it's already there.

### Step 4: Confirm the file exists

```bash
ls assets
```

You must see:

```text
alarm-tone.mp3
```

alongside the existing icon files. If it's not listed, the upload didn't land in the right folder — repeat Step 2.

### Step 5: Restart Expo

```bash
npx expo start --tunnel --clear
```

The `--clear` flag forces Metro to rebuild its cache now that a new asset exists. Without it, Metro may still report the old "unresolved" error even after the file is added.

## If it still fails after this

Send me the exact output of:

```bash
ls assets
```

and the new error text, if any. Do not change any code — the `RingingScreen.tsx` and `AlarmScreen.tsx` files from my last message are correct as written; this is purely a missing-asset issue.

# ERROR \[Error: Call to function 'AudioPlayer.pause' has been rejected.

→ Caused by: The 1st argument cannot be cast to type class expo.modules.audio.AudioPlayer (received class java.lang.Integer)
→ Caused by: Cannot use shared object that was already released\]

This is a classic React Native lifecycle bug with `expo-audio`'s shared native object. `useAudioPlayer` releases its own native player when `RingingScreen` unmounts (which happens the instant `dismissWake()` fires and `App.tsx` swaps back to the tab navigator). Your own effect then *also* tries to call `player.pause()` in its cleanup — but by then the native player is already gone. That's the "already released" crash.

**Fix: stop calling `.pause()` in the cleanup function.** The hook's own unmount teardown already kills the sound — you don't need to pause it yourself on unmount.

## Exact change — only one spot in `RingingScreen.tsx`

Find this block:

```tsx
useEffect(() => {
  if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing') {
    player.loop = true;
    player.seekTo(0);
    player.play();
  } else {
    player.pause();
  }

  return () => {
    player.pause();
  };
}, [wakeFlow.status]);
```

Replace it with exactly this:

```tsx
useEffect(() => {
  try {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing') {
      player.loop = true;
      player.seekTo(0);
      player.play();
    } else {
      player.pause();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // Player may already be released if the screen is unmounting. Safe to ignore.
  }
}, [wakeFlow.status]);
```

Two changes:

1. Removed the `return () => { player.pause(); }` cleanup entirely — that was the line causing the crash.
2. Wrapped the body in `try/catch` as a safety net for any other rapid status-flip edge case.

Nothing else in the file changes. Save it, then:

```bash
npx expo start --tunnel --clear
```

Test the full loop again: arm → preview → ring with sound/vibration → snooze → wait for return-to-ringing → scan barcode → dismiss. Confirm no red error appears in the terminal when the alarm dismisses. If it's clean, commit:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Fix audio player crash on wake flow dismissal"
git push origin main
```

Then proceed to the APK build steps from before.

## Replace all of `src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Pressable, Modal } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import { Accelerometer } from 'expo-sensors';
import { useAudioPlayer } from 'expo-audio';
import * as Haptics from 'expo-haptics';
import { colors } from '../theme/colors';
import { useSettings } from '../context/SettingsContext';
import { useWakeFlow } from '../context/WakeFlowContext';

const SNOOZE_PIN = '8492017356';

export default function RingingScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const {
    wakeFlow,
    incrementMovement,
    dismissWake,
    startSnooze,
    decrementSnooze,
    returnToRingingFromSnooze,
  } = useWakeFlow();

  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(new Date());
  const [sensorAvailable, setSensorAvailable] = useState(true);

  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [wrongBarcodeMessage, setWrongBarcodeMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const scanLockRef = useRef(false);

  const [isKeypadOpen, setIsKeypadOpen] = useState(false);
  const [enteredPin, setEnteredPin] = useState('');
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState(false);

  const lastAccelRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; z: number } | null>(null);
  const lastMovementTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const progressRef = useRef(wakeFlow.movementProgress);

  const player = useAudioPlayer(require('../../assets/alarm-tone.mp3'));

  useEffect(() => {
    progressRef.current = wakeFlow.movementProgress;
  }, [wakeFlow.movementProgress]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => setCurrentTime(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing') {
        player.loop = true;
        player.seekTo(0);
        player.play();
      } else {
        player.pause();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      // Player may already be released if the screen is unmounting. Safe to ignore.
    }
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'ringing') return;

    Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Warning);

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Warning);
    }, 2800);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  const isMovementComplete = !settings.movementEnabled || wakeFlow.movementProgress >= settings.movementTarget;

  useEffect(() => {
    let subscription: { remove: () => void } | null = null;
    let isActive = true;

    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'ringing' || !settings.movementEnabled) {
      return;
    }

    const setup = async () => {
      const available = await Accelerometer.isAvailableAsync();
      if (!isActive) return;
      setSensorAvailable(available);
      if (!available) return;

      Accelerometer.setUpdateInterval(120);
      subscription = Accelerometer.addListener((data: { x: number; y: number; z: number }) => {
        const { x, y, z } = data;
        const last = lastAccelRef.current;

        if (last) {
          const delta = Math.abs(x - last.x) + Math.abs(y - last.y) + Math.abs(z - last.z);
          const now = Date.now();

          if (delta > 0.7 && now - lastMovementTimeRef.current > 200 && progressRef.current < settings.movementTarget) {
            lastMovementTimeRef.current = now;
            incrementMovement(settings.movementTarget);
          }
        }

        lastAccelRef.current = { x, y, z };
      });
    };

    setup();

    return () => {
      isActive = false;
      if (subscription) {
        subscription.remove();
      }
    };
  }, [wakeFlow.status, settings.movementEnabled, settings.movementTarget]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status !== 'snoozed') return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      decrementSnooze();
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [wakeFlow.status]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'snoozed' && wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining === 0) {
      returnToRingingFromSnooze();
    }
  }, [wakeFlow.status, wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining]);

  const handleOpenDisarmScanner = async () => {
    if (wakeFlow.status === 'ringing' && !isMovementComplete) return;

    scanLockRef.current = false;
    setWrongBarcodeMessage(null);

    if (!permission?.granted) {
      const result = await requestPermission();
      if (!result.granted) {
        setIsScannerOpen(true);
        return;
      }
    }

    setIsScannerOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDisarmBarcodeScanned = (result: { data: string }) => {
    if (scanLockRef.current) return;

    const scanned = result.data.trim();
    if (!scanned) return;

    if (scanned === settings.wakeBarcode) {
      scanLockRef.current = true;
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Success);
      setIsScannerOpen(false);
      dismissWake();
      return;
    }

    scanLockRef.current = true;
    Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Error);
    setWrongBarcodeMessage(`Wrong item. Scan your ${settings.wakeItemName || 'registered wake-up item'}.`);
    setTimeout(() => {
      scanLockRef.current = false;
    }, 1500);
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (digit: string) => {
    if (enteredPin.length < 10) {
      setEnteredPin((prev) => prev + digit);
      setPinError(false);
    }
  };

  const handleClearPin = () => {
    setEnteredPin('');
    setPinError(false);
  };

  const handleConfirmPin = () => {
    if (enteredPin === SNOOZE_PIN) {
      Haptics.impactAsync(Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Medium);
      setEnteredPin('');
      setPinError(false);
      setIsKeypadOpen(false);
      startSnooze();
    } else {
      Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Error);
      setPinError(true);
      setEnteredPin('');
    }
  };

  const timeFormatted = currentTime.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false });
  const minutes = Math.floor(wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining / 60);
  const seconds = wakeFlow.snoozeSecondsRemaining % 60;
  const snoozeTimeFormatted = `${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top, paddingBottom: insets.bottom }]}>
      {wakeFlow.status === 'ringing' ? (
        <>
          <View style={styles.topSection}>
            <View style={styles.statusPill}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>Alarm Active</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.clockText}>{timeFormatted}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subText}>
              Scan <Text style={styles.subTextBold}>{settings.wakeItemName || 'your registered item'}</Text> to
              disarm
            </Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.middleSection}>
            {settings.movementEnabled && (
              <View style={styles.card}>
                <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Physical Movement</Text>

                {sensorAvailable ? (
                  <>
                    <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
                      {wakeFlow.movementProgress} / {settings.movementTarget}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.progressFill,
                          { width: `${Math.min(100, (wakeFlow.movementProgress / settings.movementTarget) * 100)}%` },
                        ]}
                      />
                    </View>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Motion sensor unavailable. Use manual fallback.</Text>
                    <Pressable
                      style={styles.fallbackButton}
                      onPress={() => incrementMovement(settings.movementTarget)}
                      accessibilityLabel="Simulate movement, sensor unavailable"
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.fallbackButtonText}>
                        Tap to simulate movement ({wakeFlow.movementProgress}/{settings.movementTarget})
                      </Text>
                    </Pressable>
                  </>
                )}
              </View>
            )}

            {wrongBarcodeMessage && (
              <View style={styles.errorBanner}>
                <Text style={styles.errorBannerText}>{wrongBarcodeMessage}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.scanButton, !isMovementComplete && styles.scanButtonDisabled]}
              onPress={handleOpenDisarmScanner}
              disabled={!isMovementComplete}
              accessibilityLabel="Scan to Disarm"
            >
              <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>
                {isMovementComplete ? 'Scan to Disarm' : 'Complete Movement First'}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.snoozeButton}
              onPress={() => setIsKeypadOpen(true)}
              accessibilityLabel="Snooze with PIN"
            >
              <Text style={styles.snoozeButtonText}>Snooze with PIN</Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.exitPreviewButton}
              onPress={dismissWake}
              accessibilityLabel="Exit preview, testing only"
            >
              <Text style={styles.exitPreviewButtonText}>Exit Preview (Testing Only)</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <View style={styles.topSection}>
            <View style={[styles.statusPill, styles.statusPillSnooze]}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>Snoozed</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.snoozeCountdownText}>{snoozeTimeFormatted}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subText}>Alarm will ring again in</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.middleSection}>
            {wrongBarcodeMessage && (
              <View style={styles.errorBanner}>
                <Text style={styles.errorBannerText}>{wrongBarcodeMessage}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomSection}>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.scanButton}
              onPress={handleOpenDisarmScanner}
              accessibilityLabel="Scan to Disarm Now"
            >
              <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>Scan to Disarm Now</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </>
      )}

      <Modal
        visible={isScannerOpen}
        animationType="slide"
        transparent={false}
        onRequestClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { paddingTop: insets.top, paddingBottom: insets.bottom }]}>
          {!permission?.granted ? (
            <View style={styles.permissionContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.permissionTitle}>Camera Permission Required</Text>
              <Text style={styles.permissionText}>Camera access is needed to scan your wake-up item.</Text>

              <View style={styles.modalFooter}>
                <Pressable
                  style={styles.cancelButton}
                  onPress={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.cameraWrapper}>
              <CameraView
                style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
                facing="back"
                barcodeScannerSettings={{
                  barcodeTypes: ['ean13', 'ean8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e', 'code128', 'code39', 'qr'],
                }}
                onBarcodeScanned={handleDisarmBarcodeScanned}
              />

              <View style={styles.cameraOverlay} pointerEvents="box-none">
                <View style={styles.cameraHeaderBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraHeaderText}>Scan {settings.wakeItemName}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraSubHeaderText}>Position the barcode in the center</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.cameraFooter}>
                  <Pressable
                    style={styles.cancelButton}
                    onPress={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
                    accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        visible={isKeypadOpen}
        animationType="fade"
        transparent={true}
        onRequestClose={() => setIsKeypadOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.keypadOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.keypadContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.keypadTitle}>Snooze PIN</Text>

            <View style={[styles.pinDisplay, pinError && styles.pinDisplayError]}>
              <Text style={styles.pinDisplayText}>{enteredPin.padEnd(10, '·')}</Text>
            </View>

            {pinError && <Text style={styles.pinErrorText}>Incorrect PIN. Please try again.</Text>}

            <View style={styles.keypadGrid}>
              {['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9'].map((digit) => (
                <Pressable
                  key={digit}
                  style={styles.keypadKey}
                  onPress={() => handleKeyPress(digit)}
                  accessibilityLabel={`Digit ${digit}`}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.keypadKeyText}>{digit}</Text>
                </Pressable>
              ))}

              <Pressable style={styles.keypadKeyClear} onPress={handleClearPin} accessibilityLabel="Clear PIN">
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyClearText}>CLEAR</Text>
              </Pressable>

              <Pressable style={styles.keypadKey} onPress={() => handleKeyPress('0')} accessibilityLabel="Digit 0">
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyText}>0</Text>
              </Pressable>

              <Pressable
                style={[styles.keypadKeyConfirm, enteredPin.length !== 10 && styles.keypadKeyConfirmDisabled]}
                onPress={handleConfirmPin}
                disabled={enteredPin.length !== 10}
                accessibilityLabel="Confirm PIN"
              >
                <Text style={styles.keypadKeyConfirmText}>OK</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </View>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.keypadCancel}
              onPress={() => {
                setIsKeypadOpen(false);
                setEnteredPin('');
                setPinError(false);
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Cancel snooze"
            >
              <Text style={styles.keypadCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: 20 },
  topSection: { alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 24 },
  statusPill: { backgroundColor: colors.card, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 20, marginBottom: 16 },

  errorBannerText: { color: colors.roseAccent, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'center' },
  bottomSection: { paddingBottom: 12, gap: 10 },

  scanButtonDisabled: { backgroundColor: '#333333', opacity: 0.7 },

  cameraSubHeaderText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14 },

  pinDisplayError: { borderColor: colors.roseAccent, borderWidth: 1 },
  pinDisplayText: { color: colors.amberAccent, textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold', letterSpacing: 4 },
  pinErrorText: { color: colors.roseAccent, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  keypadGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 },
  keypadKey: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: '#222', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  keypadKeyText: { color: colors.text, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  keypadKeyClear: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: '#1a1a1a', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  keypadKeyClearText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  keypadKeyConfirm: { width: '30%', height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },

  keypadCancel: { marginTop: 16, alignItems: 'center' },
  keypadCancelText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 'bold' },
});
```

Two separate things here: the snooze PIN is currently **hardcoded** (`8492017356`) with no way to set your own, and your `app.json` is missing two required pieces for a standalone build. Fixing both now — 4 files total, so full replacements per your rule.

## Fix 1: Let yourself set your own snooze PIN

## Replace all of `src/context/SettingsContext.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { createContext, useState, useEffect, useContext, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { colors } from '../theme/colors';

export type AlarmSettings = {
  alarmHour: number;
  alarmMinute: number;
  repeatDays: number[];
  isArmed: boolean;
  wakeItemName: string;
  wakeBarcode: string;
  snoozePin: string;
  movementEnabled: boolean;
  movementTarget: number;
};

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: AlarmSettings = {
  alarmHour: 7,
  alarmMinute: 0,
  repeatDays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
  isArmed: false,
  wakeItemName: 'Bathroom Toothpaste',
  wakeBarcode: '012345678905',
  snoozePin: '8492017356',
  movementEnabled: true,
  movementTarget: 15,
};

function isValidSettings(data: any): data is Partial<AlarmSettings> {
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return false;

  if ('alarmHour' in data) {
    if (typeof data.alarmHour !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(data.alarmHour) || data.alarmHour < 0 || data.alarmHour > 23) return false;
  }
  if ('alarmMinute' in data) {
    if (typeof data.alarmMinute !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(data.alarmMinute) || data.alarmMinute < 0 || data.alarmMinute > 59) return false;
  }
  if ('repeatDays' in data) {
    if (!Array.isArray(data.repeatDays)) return false;
    for (const day of data.repeatDays) {
      if (typeof day !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 0 || day > 6) return false;
    }
  }
  if ('isArmed' in data && typeof data.isArmed !== 'boolean') return false;
  if ('wakeItemName' in data && typeof data.wakeItemName !== 'string') return false;
  if ('wakeBarcode' in data && typeof data.wakeBarcode !== 'string') return false;
  if ('snoozePin' in data) {
    if (typeof data.snoozePin !== 'string' || !/^\d{10}$/.test(data.snoozePin)) return false;
  }
  if ('movementEnabled' in data && typeof data.movementEnabled !== 'boolean') return false;
  if ('movementTarget' in data) {
    if (typeof data.movementTarget !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(data.movementTarget) || !Number.isInteger(data.movementTarget) || data.movementTarget <= 0) return false;
  }

  return true;
}

type SettingsContextType = {
  settings: AlarmSettings;
  updateSettings: (newSettings: Partial<AlarmSettings>) => void;
  resetSettings: () => void;
};

const SettingsContext = createContext<SettingsContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function SettingsProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AlarmSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSettings();
  }, []);

  const loadSettings = async () => {
    try {
      const storedSettings = await AsyncStorage.getItem('@wakeup_settings');
      if (storedSettings !== null) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(storedSettings);
        if (isValidSettings(parsed)) {
          setSettings({
            ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
            ...parsed,
            // Native alarm scheduling will own armed state in a later stage.
            isArmed: false,
          });
        } else {
          setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load settings', error);
      setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const updateSettings = (newSettings: Partial<AlarmSettings>) => {
    setSettings((previousSettings) => {
      const updatedSettings = {
        ...previousSettings,
        ...newSettings,
      };

      AsyncStorage.setItem('@wakeup_settings', JSON.stringify(updatedSettings))
        .catch((error) => console.error('Failed to save settings', error));

      return updatedSettings;
    });
  };

  const resetSettings = async () => {
    try {
      setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      await AsyncStorage.setItem('@wakeup_settings', JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_SETTINGS));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to reset settings', error);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.loadingContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading settings...</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SettingsContext.Provider value={{ settings, updateSettings, resetSettings }}>
      {children}
    </SettingsContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useSettings() {
  const context = useContext(SettingsContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useSettings must be used within a SettingsProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  loadingContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: colors.background,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  loadingText: {
    color: colors.textSecondary,
    fontSize: 16,
  },
});
```


## Replace all of `src/screens/SetupScreen.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TextInput, Pressable, Modal, Linking } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import { colors } from '../theme/colors';
import { useSettings } from '../context/SettingsContext';

function validateSnoozePin(pin: string): { isValid: boolean; error?: string } {
  if (!/^\d{10}$/.test(pin)) {
    return { isValid: false, error: 'PIN must be exactly 10 digits.' };
  }

  if (/^(\d)\1{9}$/.test(pin)) {
    return { isValid: false, error: 'Avoid using the same digit repeated 10 times.' };
  }

  const sequentialAsc = '01234567890123456789';
  const sequentialDesc = '98765432109876543210';
  if (sequentialAsc.includes(pin) || sequentialDesc.includes(pin)) {
    return { isValid: false, error: 'Avoid simple sequential numbers.' };
  }

  const pattern2 = pin.slice(0, 2);
  if (pattern2.repeat(5) === pin) {
    return { isValid: false, error: 'Avoid simple repeating patterns.' };
  }

  const uniqueDigits = new Set(pin.split('')).size;
  if (uniqueDigits < 4) {
    return { isValid: false, error: `Use at least 4 different digits (currently ${uniqueDigits}).` };
  }

  return { isValid: true };
}

export default function SetupScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { settings, updateSettings } = useSettings();

  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const [isScanning, setIsScanning] = useState(false);
  const [scanned, setScanned] = useState(false);
  const scanLockRef = useRef(false);
  const [showSuccess, setShowSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [manualBarcode, setManualBarcode] = useState('');
  const [retryCount, setRetryCount] = useState(0);

  const [pinDraft, setPinDraft] = useState(settings.snoozePin);
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pinSaved, setPinSaved] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPinDraft(settings.snoozePin);
  }, [settings.snoozePin]);

  const isBarcodeConfigured = Boolean(settings.wakeBarcode && settings.wakeBarcode.length > 0);
  const lastFour = isBarcodeConfigured ? settings.wakeBarcode.slice(-4) : '';

  const handleOpenScanner = async () => {
    scanLockRef.current = false;
    setScanned(false);

    if (!permission?.granted) {
      const result = await requestPermission();

      if (!result.granted) {
        setIsScanning(true);
        return;
      }
    }

    setIsScanning(true);
  };

  const handleBarcodeDetected = (code: string) => {
    if (scanLockRef.current) {
      return;
    }

    const trimmed = code.trim();

    if (!trimmed) {
      return;
    }

    scanLockRef.current = true;
    setScanned(true);
    updateSettings({ wakeBarcode: trimmed });
    setIsScanning(false);
    triggerSuccess();
  };

  const handleManualSubmit = () => {
    if (!manualBarcode.trim()) return;
    handleBarcodeDetected(manualBarcode.trim());
    setManualBarcode('');
  };

  const triggerSuccess = () => {
    setShowSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setShowSuccess(false);
    }, 4000);
  };

  const handleRetryCamera = () => {
    setRetryCount((prev) => prev + 1);
  };

  const adjustMovement = (delta: number) => {
    let newTarget = settings.movementTarget + delta;
    if (newTarget < 5) newTarget = 5;
    if (newTarget > 50) newTarget = 50;
    updateSettings({ movementTarget: newTarget });
  };

  const handleSavePin = () => {
    const result = validateSnoozePin(pinDraft);

    if (!result.isValid) {
      setPinError(result.error || 'Invalid PIN.');
      return;
    }

    updateSettings({ snoozePin: pinDraft });
    setPinError(null);
    setPinSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setPinSaved(false), 3000);
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
        <Text style={styles.header}>Mission Setup</Text>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Wake-up Item</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardDescription}>
            Name the physical item you will scan to turn off the alarm.
          </Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={settings.wakeItemName}
            onChangeText={(text) => updateSettings({ wakeItemName: text })}
            placeholder="e.g. Bathroom Toothpaste"
            placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary}
            accessibilityLabel="Wake-up item name"
          />
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Barcode Mission</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardDescription}>
            Scan the barcode on your {settings.wakeItemName || 'item'} to wake up.
          </Text>

          {isBarcodeConfigured ? (
            <View style={styles.statusBox}>
              <Text style={styles.statusConfigured}>Barcode configured</Text>
              <Text style={styles.statusDetail}>Saved barcode ending in {lastFour}</Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <Text style={styles.statusMissing}>No barcode configured</Text>
          )}

          {showSuccess && (
            <View style={styles.successBadge}>
              <Text style={styles.successText}>Wake-up item barcode saved</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <Pressable
            style={styles.scanButton}
            onPress={handleOpenScanner}
            accessibilityLabel="Scan Wake-up Item Barcode"
          >
            <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>Scan Wake-up Item Barcode</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Snooze PIN</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardDescription}>
            Enter this 10-digit code during the alarm to snooze instead of scanning right away.
          </Text>

          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={pinDraft}
            onChangeText={(text) => {
              const digitsOnly = text.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 10);
              setPinDraft(digitsOnly);
              setPinError(null);
            }}
            placeholder="Enter 10-digit PIN"
            placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary}
            keyboardType="number-pad"
            maxLength={10}
            accessibilityLabel="Snooze PIN"
          />

          {pinError && <Text style={styles.statusMissing}>{pinError}</Text>}

          {pinSaved && (
            <View style={styles.successBadge}>
              <Text style={styles.successText}>Snooze PIN saved</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.scanButton, pinDraft.length !== 10 && styles.scanButtonDisabled]}
            onPress={handleSavePin}
            disabled={pinDraft.length !== 10}
            accessibilityLabel="Save snooze PIN"
          >
            <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>Save PIN</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Movement Mission</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardDescription}>
            Walk steps away from your bed to dismiss the alarm.
          </Text>

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.toggleButton, settings.movementEnabled && styles.toggleButtonActive]}
            onPress={() => updateSettings({ movementEnabled: !settings.movementEnabled })}
            accessibilityLabel={`Movement mission is ${settings.movementEnabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}. Tap to toggle.`}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.toggleText, settings.movementEnabled && styles.toggleTextActive]}>
              {settings.movementEnabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>

          {settings.movementEnabled && (
            <View style={styles.targetContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.targetLabel}>Movement target: {settings.movementTarget}</Text>
              <View style={styles.targetControls}>
                <Pressable
                  style={styles.adjustBtn}
                  onPress={() => adjustMovement(-1)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Decrease movement target"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.adjustBtnText}>-</Text>
                </Pressable>
                <Text style={styles.targetValue}>{settings.movementTarget}</Text>
                <Pressable
                  style={styles.adjustBtn}
                  onPress={() => adjustMovement(1)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Increase movement target"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.adjustBtnText}>+</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>

      <Modal visible={isScanning} animationType="slide" transparent={false} onRequestClose={() => setIsScanning(false)}>
        <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { paddingTop: insets.top, paddingBottom: insets.bottom }]}>
          {!permission?.granted ? (
            <View style={styles.permissionContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.permissionTitle}>Camera Permission Required</Text>
              <Text style={styles.permissionText}>Camera access is needed to scan your wake-up item.</Text>

              <Pressable
                style={styles.blueButton}
                onPress={() => Linking.openSettings()}
                accessibilityLabel="Open app settings"
              >
                <Text style={styles.blueButtonText}>Open Settings</Text>
              </Pressable>

              <View style={styles.fallbackContainer}>
                <View style={styles.fallbackHeader}>
                  <Text style={styles.fallbackTitle}>Accessibility fallback</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.fallbackDesc}>
                    Use this only when camera access is unavailable. It should not be used to bypass the normal
                    physical scanning mission.
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.input}
                  value={manualBarcode}
                  onChangeText={setManualBarcode}
                  placeholder="Enter registered barcode"
                  placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary}
                  autoCapitalize="characters"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  accessibilityLabel="Manual barcode input"
                />
                <Pressable
                  style={[styles.scanButton, !manualBarcode.trim() && styles.scanButtonDisabled]}
                  onPress={handleManualSubmit}
                  disabled={!manualBarcode.trim()}
                  accessibilityLabel="Save manual barcode"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.scanButtonText}>Save Manual Barcode</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.modalFooter}>
                <Pressable
                  style={styles.cancelButton}
                  onPress={() => setIsScanning(false)}
                  accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                >
                  <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.cameraWrapper}>
              <CameraView
                style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
                facing="back"
                barcodeScannerSettings={{
                  barcodeTypes: ['ean13', 'ean8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e', 'code128', 'code39', 'qr'],
                }}
                onBarcodeScanned={scanned ? undefined : (result) => handleBarcodeDetected(result.data)}
              />

              <View style={styles.cameraOverlay} pointerEvents="box-none">
                <View style={styles.cameraHeaderBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraHeaderText}>Scan {settings.wakeItemName}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.cameraSubHeaderText}>Position the barcode in the center</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.cameraFooter}>
                  <Pressable
                    style={styles.cancelButton}
                    onPress={() => setIsScanning(false)}
                    accessibilityLabel="Cancel barcode scanning"
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>Cancel</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  scrollContent: { paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingBottom: 40 },
  header: { fontSize: 32, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginVertical: 24 },
  card: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 24, padding: 24, marginBottom: 16 },
  cardTitle: { fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 'bold', color: colors.text, marginBottom: 8 },
  cardDescription: { fontSize: 14, color: colors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 16, lineHeight: 20 },
  input: { backgroundColor: colors.background, color: colors.text, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#333' },

  statusBox: { marginBottom: 16 },
  statusConfigured: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 4 },
  statusDetail: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14 },
  statusMissing: { color: colors.roseAccent, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 12 },

  successBadge: { backgroundColor: colors.emeraldAccent, padding: 12, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 16, alignItems: 'center' },
  successText: { color: '#000000', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 14 },

  scanButton: { backgroundColor: colors.amberAccent, borderRadius: 16, paddingVertical: 16, alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 8 },
  scanButtonDisabled: { opacity: 0.5 },
  scanButtonText: { color: '#000000', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold' },

  toggleButton: { alignSelf: 'flex-start', backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingVertical: 10, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#333' },
  toggleButtonActive: { backgroundColor: colors.blueAccent, borderColor: colors.blueAccent },
  toggleText: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  toggleTextActive: { color: '#ffffff' },

  targetContainer: { marginTop: 24, padding: 16, backgroundColor: colors.background, borderRadius: 16 },
  targetLabel: { color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 12, textAlign: 'center' },
  targetControls: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  adjustBtn: { width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, backgroundColor: colors.card, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  adjustBtnText: { color: colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 'bold' },
  targetValue: { color: colors.text, fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 'bold', marginHorizontal: 24, width: 40, textAlign: 'center' },
```

## In `src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx` — one small edit

Find:

```tsx
const SNOOZE_PIN = '8492017356';
```

Delete that entire line.

Find:

```tsx
if (enteredPin === SNOOZE_PIN) {
```

Replace with:

```tsx
if (enteredPin === settings.snoozePin) {
```

That's the only change needed in that file — `settings` is already available in that component.

## Fix 2: `app.json`

Two things are missing: an Android package ID (required to build) and the `expo-camera` permission plugin (required or camera will break in the standalone APK even though it works in Expo Go). Replace the whole file:

```json
{
  "expo": {
    "name": "alarmG-native",
    "slug": "alarmG-native",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "orientation": "portrait",
    "icon": "./assets/icon.png",
    "userInterfaceStyle": "dark",
    "ios": {
      "supportsTablet": true
    },
    "android": {
      "package": "com.rainbowblurry.wakeupnative",
      "adaptiveIcon": {
        "backgroundColor": "#0a0a0a",
        "foregroundImage": "./assets/android-icon-foreground.png",
        "backgroundImage": "./assets/android-icon-background.png",
        "monochromeImage": "./assets/android-icon-monochrome.png"
      },
      "predictiveBackGestureEnabled": false
    },
    "web": {
      "favicon": "./assets/favicon.png"
    },
    "plugins": [
      "expo-audio",
      [
        "expo-camera",
        {
          "cameraPermission": "WakeUp needs camera access to scan your registered wake-up item barcode."
        }
      ]
    ]
  }
}
```

Two cosmetic changes I made while I was in there: `userInterfaceStyle` to `"dark"` (matches the app so there's no white flash before JS loads) and `adaptiveIcon.backgroundColor` to dark (was a mismatched light blue). Both harmless, not required, feel free to change the color back if you'd rather pick your own icon background later.

## Run it

```bash
npx tsc --noEmit
npx expo start --tunnel --clear
```

Go to Setup, set your own PIN (try one that passes validation, e.g. `1937284650`), save it, then arm → preview → snooze with your new PIN instead of the old default.

## Commit

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Add configurable snooze PIN and fix app.json for build"
git push origin main
```

Then proceed to the `eas build:configure` / `eas build --platform android --profile preview` steps from before.

# Cannot find name 'settings'. in pin

I need the exact file and line to pin this down — "Cannot find name 'settings'" is a TypeScript error, and it'll say exactly where. Please paste the full terminal output (it'll look like `error TS2304: Cannot find name 'settings'.` with a file path and line number above it).

While you grab that, the most likely cause: `settings` is only in scope if the component actually destructures it from `useSettings()`. Check this now in **`src/screens/RingingScreen.tsx`**:

Near the top of the `RingingScreen` function, you should see:

```tsx
const { settings } = useSettings();
```

right before or after:

```tsx
const {
  wakeFlow,
  incrementMovement,
  dismissWake,
  startSnooze,
  decrementSnooze,
  returnToRingingFromSnooze,
} = useWakeFlow();
```

If that `const { settings } = useSettings();` line is missing — for example if it got deleted or wasn't there when you made the small two-line edit — that's exactly why `settings.snoozePin` fails inside `handleConfirmPin`.

## Quick fix if that line is missing

Add it back exactly as shown above, directly under the function's opening line:

```tsx
export default function RingingScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const {
    wakeFlow,
    incrementMovement,
    dismissWake,
    startSnooze,
    decrementSnooze,
    returnToRingingFromSnooze,
  } = useWakeFlow();
```

Save, then:

```bash
npx tsc --noEmit
```

If it comes back clean, run Expo again. If the error is actually in a different file (e.g. `SetupScreen.tsx`), send me that exact `file:line` and I'll fix that specific spot.
